# Shell Amulet

This piece of content contains information obtained through datamining.

Due to the nature of the information, details may be inaccurate or outdated.

Datamined information: Shell Amulet's possible stats and relevant information. — December 19th, 2024

The **Shell Amulet** is an [amulet](amulet.md) given by defeating the [Stump Snail](stump-snail.md) in the [Stump Field](stump-field.md). The amulet's tier varies, with higher tiers being unlocked once the player reaches a certain amount of Stump Snail defeats.

The amulets grant buffs to the owner and always increase [goo](goo.md) and [Stump Field](stump-field.md) pollen as they go up a tier. Higher tier amulets grant more and better buffs.

The chance of getting an amulet of a higher tier and the quality of the amulet increases linearly with the number of Stump Snail kills up to an unknown number, where its quality is maxed.

## Trivia

* The Shell Amulet is the second amulet to be dropped by a mob with the first amulet being the [King Beetle Amulet](king-beetle-amulet.md) which is dropped by [King Beetle](king-beetle.md).
* The Shell Amulet is the only amulet to boost [goo](goo.md).
  * It is also the only amulet to boost [goo conversion](system-page.md#Goo_Conversion) and [defense](system-page.md#Defense).
